# Malayalam alphabet: exact voice labels

Generated by `tools/s2w_alphabet.py markdown`; do not edit by hand (a test fails when this file is out of date).

**Status: a curated reference, not verified.** The tags, say-as wording, IPA and example words were written for this project and need confirming by a native speaker listening (use the verdict buttons on the recorder's `/alphabet` page). Machine-generated audio is not evidence of any of it.

## What is counted

- 5596 entries: 16 independent vowel letters, 2 vowel + anusvara/visarga, 576 consonant forms (36 modern consonant letters x 16 forms: virama, inherent a, 12 vowel signs, anusvara, visarga), 6 chillu letters, 2119 conjunct consonants x 13 forms (ങ്ങ = ngga, ക്ക = kka, ...), 2877 modified forms (sign + anusvara, conjunct + chandrakkala / anusvara / visarga; in alphabet.csv).
- Identity is the Malayalam text / code points. `sound_id` = `ml-` + code points + Manglish + a tag when the Manglish is shared, so it is unique for every entry.
- 2745 entries share their Manglish with a different letter. The 13 base groups are below; every consonant+vowel form of a group collides too.

## Same Manglish, different letters

| Manglish | letters | exact labels |
|---|---|---|
| da (consonant letters) | ഡ ദ | ഡ `ml-0d21-da-retroflex`<br>ദ `ml-0d26-da-dental` |
| dha (consonant letters) | ഢ ധ | ഢ `ml-0d22-dha-retroflex`<br>ധ `ml-0d27-dha-dental` |
| la (consonant letters) | ല ള | ല `ml-0d32-la-plain`<br>ള `ml-0d33-la-retroflex` |
| na (consonant letters) | ണ ന | ണ `ml-0d23-na-retroflex`<br>ന `ml-0d28-na-dental` |
| ra (consonant letters) | ര റ | ര `ml-0d30-ra-tap`<br>റ `ml-0d31-ra-trill` |
| sha (consonant letters) | ശ ഷ | ശ `ml-0d36-sha-soft`<br>ഷ `ml-0d37-sha-hard` |
| tha (consonant letters) | ഠ ത ഥ | ഠ `ml-0d20-tha-retroflex-aspirated`<br>ത `ml-0d24-tha-dental`<br>ഥ `ml-0d25-tha-aspirated` |
| e (vowel letters) | എ ഏ | എ `ml-0d0e-e-short`<br>ഏ `ml-0d0f-e-long` |
| o (vowel letters) | ഒ ഓ | ഒ `ml-0d12-o-short`<br>ഓ `ml-0d13-o-long` |
| ee (vowel signs) | ീ േ | കീ `ml-0d15-0d40-kee-long-i`<br>കേ `ml-0d15-0d47-kee-long-e` |
| oo (vowel signs) | ൂ ോ | കൂ `ml-0d15-0d42-koo-long-u`<br>കോ `ml-0d15-0d4b-koo-long-o` |
| l (chillu letters) | ൽ ൾ | ൽ `ml-0d7d-l-plain`<br>ൾ `ml-0d7e-l-retroflex` |
| n (chillu letters) | ൺ ൻ | ൺ `ml-0d7a-n-retroflex`<br>ൻ `ml-0d7b-n-dental` |

Acoustic neighbours with different Manglish: sibilants: ശ ഷ സ; nasals: ന ണ ങ ഞ; rhotics: ര റ; laterals: ല ള ഴ; t: ത ട ഥ ഠ; d: ദ ഡ ധ ഢ.
